title: GCP IAM Permissions Reference
description: The 901 read-only permissions in the AccuKnox CSPM GCP custom role, with the reason for each.
hide:
  - toc
---

# GCP Permissions Reference

AccuKnox's GCP scanner uses a **project-level custom role with 901 read-only permissions** across 41 services. Every permission is a `.list`, `.get`, `.getIamPolicy`, or export/analyze verb, so AccuKnox never reads object contents, table rows, or secret payloads.

Every permission is listed below, grouped by service. See the [overview](cspm-permissions-overview.md) to compare clouds, or the [GCP prerequisites](cspm-prereq-gcp.md) for setup steps.

!!! note "About the `iam.googleapis.com/*` permissions"
    17 permissions use the fully-qualified `iam.googleapis.com/*` form (Workload Identity Federation and OAuth clients). These are not part of the built-in `roles/viewer` and are included deliberately for full IAM posture visibility.


## Access Approval (4)

| Permission | Why we need it | Findings / asset discovery |
|---|---|---|
| `accessapproval.requests.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of requests to evaluate posture rules against it. Read Access Approval settings and requests. | Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |
| `accessapproval.requests.list` | Enumerates requests so they appear in the asset inventory. Read Access Approval settings and requests. | Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |
| `accessapproval.serviceAccounts.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of service accounts to evaluate posture rules against it. Read Access Approval settings and requests. | Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |
| `accessapproval.settings.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of settings to evaluate posture rules against it. Read Access Approval settings and requests. | Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |


## API Gateway (4)

| Permission | Why we need it | Findings / asset discovery |
|---|---|---|
| `apigateway.apiconfigs.list` | Enumerates apiconfigs so they appear in the asset inventory. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy. | Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |
| `apigateway.apis.list` | Enumerates apis so they appear in the asset inventory. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy. | Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |
| `apigateway.gateways.getIamPolicy` | Reads the IAM policy attached to gateways to detect public or over-permissive access. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy. | Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |
| `apigateway.gateways.list` | Enumerates gateways so they appear in the asset inventory. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy. | Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |


## API Keys (3)

| Permission | Why we need it | Findings / asset discovery |
|---|---|---|
| `apikeys.keys.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of keys to evaluate posture rules against it. List API keys and read their restrictions and creation time. | API Key Rotation, API Key API Restriction, API Key Application Restriction, API Key Active Services Only. Asset: gcp_apikeys_key. |
| `apikeys.keys.getKeyString` | Read-only call against keys used during the scan. List API keys and read their restrictions and creation time. | API Key Rotation, API Key API Restriction, API Key Application Restriction, API Key Active Services Only. Asset: gcp_apikeys_key. |
| `apikeys.keys.list` | Enumerates keys so they appear in the asset inventory. List API keys and read their restrictions and creation time. | API Key Rotation, API Key API Restriction, API Key Application Restriction, API Key Active Services Only. Asset: gcp_apikeys_key. |


## Artifact Registry (4)

| Permission | Why we need it | Findings / asset discovery |
|---|---|---|
| `artifactregistry.packages.list` | Enumerates packages so they appear in the asset inventory. List repositories and read their IAM policy and CMEK settings. | Repository Public Access, Repository CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_artifact_registry_repository. |
| `artifactregistry.repositories.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of repositories to evaluate posture rules against it. List repositories and read their IAM policy and CMEK settings. | Repository Public Access, Repository CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_artifact_registry_repository. |
| `artifactregistry.repositories.list` | Enumerates repositories so they appear in the asset inventory. List repositories and read their IAM policy and CMEK settings. | Repository Public Access, Repository CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_artifact_registry_repository. |
| `artifactregistry.versions.list` | Enumerates versions so they appear in the asset inventory. List repositories and read their IAM policy and CMEK settings. | Repository Public Access, Repository CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_artifact_registry_repository. |


## BigQuery (4)

| Permission | Why we need it | Findings / asset discovery |
|---|---|---|
| `bigquery.datasets.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of datasets to evaluate posture rules against it. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read. | Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |
| `bigquery.datasets.getIamPolicy` | Reads the IAM policy attached to datasets to detect public or over-permissive access. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read. | Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |
| `bigquery.tables.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of tables to evaluate posture rules against it. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read. | Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |
| `bigquery.tables.list` | Enumerates tables so they appear in the asset inventory. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read. | Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |


## Cloud Bigtable (2)

| Permission | Why we need it | Findings / asset discovery |
|---|---|---|
| `bigtable.instances.getIamPolicy` | Reads the IAM policy attached to instances to detect public or over-permissive access. List instances and read their IAM policy. | Instance public-access IAM checks. Asset: gcp_bigtable_instance. |
| `bigtable.instances.list` | Enumerates instances so they appear in the asset inventory. List instances and read their IAM policy. | Instance public-access IAM checks. Asset: gcp_bigtable_instance. |


## Cloud Billing (4)

| Permission | Why we need it | Findings / asset discovery |
|---|---|---|
| `billing.anomalies.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of anomalies to evaluate posture rules against it. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project. | Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |
| `billing.anomalies.list` | Enumerates anomalies so they appear in the asset inventory. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project. | Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |
| `billing.resourceCosts.get` | Reads the detailed configuration of resource costs to evaluate posture rules against it. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project. | Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |
| `billing.resourcebudgets.read` | Reads resourcebudgets configuration used by posture rules. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project. | Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |

## Dataflow (2)
